            if (g_classic && !ResolveClassicTarget()) {
                HsMessage(HSI_WARN, L"TARGET NOT FOUND",
                          L"Enter a running PID or process name in the classic box.\r\n"
                          L"Use * to scan every accessible process.");
                break;
            }
            StartScan();
            break;
        case HS_BTN_STOP: StopScan(); break;
        case HS_BTN_SAVE_TXT: SaveReportDialog(false); break;
        case HS_BTN_SAVE_HTML: SaveReportDialog(true); break;
        case HS_BTN_CLEAR: ClearLog(); break;
        case HS_BTN_ELEVATE: RelaunchElevated(); break;
        case HS_MODE0: SelectMode(0); break;
        case HS_MODE1: SelectMode(1); break;
        case HS_MODE2: SelectMode(2); break;
        case HS_MODE3: SelectMode(3); break;
        case HS_VERB0: SelectVerb(0); break;
        case HS_VERB1: SelectVerb(1); break;
        case HS_VERB2: SelectVerb(2); break;
        case HS_TG_FILTERALL:
            UpdateToggleAvailability();
            MarkCustomPreset();
            UpdateClassicPreview();
            if (g_tab) BuildInfoView();
            break;
        case HS_TG_STATS:
        case HS_TG_FROMBASE:
        case HS_TG_DUMP:
        case HS_TG_UNSIGNED:
        case HS_TG_METADATA:
        case HS_TG_CLRHEAP:
        case HS_TG_CLRPRVX:
        case HS_TG_WOW64:
            MarkCustomPreset();
            UpdateClassicPreview();
            if (g_tab) BuildInfoView();
            break;
        case HS_TAB_OUTPUT: ShowTab(0); break;
        case HS_TAB_CMD: ShowTab(1); break;
        case HS_TAB_DETAILS: ShowTab(2); break;
        case HS_TG_CLASSIC:
            ApplyClassicMode();
            if (g_tab) BuildInfoView();
            break;
        case HS_TG_FOCUSUNSIGNED:
            g_focusUnsigned = TgGetOn(g_hTgFocusUnsigned);
            RebuildLiveView();
            SetStatus(g_focusUnsigned ? L"FOCUS: UNSIGNED MODULES" : L"READY");
            break;
        case HS_TG_INSTA:
            TgSetOn(g_hTgInsta, true);
            OpenInstagram();
            break;
        default: break;
    }
}

void DrawComboItem(LPDRAWITEMSTRUCT dis) {
    HDC dc = dis->hDC;
    RECT rc = dis->rcItem;
    bool selected = (dis->itemState & ODS_SELECTED) != 0;
    if (dis->itemID == -1) {
        selected = false;
    }

    COLORREF bg = selected ? RGB(16, 55, 64) : Th::INPUT;
    FillSolid(dc, rc, bg);

    wchar_t text[512] = {};
    int idx = (int)dis->itemID;
    if (idx == -1) idx = (int)SendMessageW(dis->hwndItem, CB_GETCURSEL, 0, 0);
    if (idx >= 0) SendMessageW(dis->hwndItem, CB_GETLBTEXT, (WPARAM)idx, (LPARAM)text);

    SetBkMode(dc, TRANSPARENT);
    SetTextColor(dc, selected ? Th::ACCENT : Th::TEXT);
    HGDIOBJ of = SelectObject(dc, g_fBody);
    RECT tr = rc;
    tr.left += S(10);
    tr.right -= S(32);
    DrawTextW(dc, text, -1, &tr, DT_LEFT | DT_VCENTER | DT_SINGLELINE | DT_END_ELLIPSIS);
    SelectObject(dc, of);
}

LRESULT CALLBACK WndProc(HWND h, UINT msg, WPARAM w, LPARAM l) {
    switch (msg) {
        case WM_CREATE: {
            g_hWnd = h;
            CreateChildControls(h);
            RefreshProcessList();
            // Lay out from the real client area: the window may be smaller than the
            // nominal 1360x900 (DPI scaling, small screens), and laying out for the
            // nominal size clipped the right column and the footer on first open.
            RECT rc{};
            GetClientRect(h, &rc);
            LayoutControls(h, rc.right, rc.bottom);
            // Make the initial tab state explicit instead of relying on creation flags,
            // so the first paint matches what a tab switch produces.
            ShowTab(0);
            SetStatus(L"READY");
            SetTimer(h, 1, 500, nullptr);
            RpcStartWorker();
            RpcPublishIdle();
#ifdef HS_CLASSIC_DEFAULT
            // Classic flavour: open straight into the moneta64.exe-style one-row layout.
            // The toggle stays live, so the user can still switch to the full GUI.
            TgSetOn(g_hTgClassic, true);
            ApplyClassicMode();
            if (g_tab) BuildInfoView();
            SetStatus(L"READY - CLASSIC");
#endif
            return 0;
        }
        case WM_SIZE: {
            if (w != SIZE_MINIMIZED) {
                RECT rc;
                GetClientRect(h, &rc);
                LayoutControls(h, rc.right, rc.bottom);
                // Repaint the frame and all children so a resize never leaves a
                // strip of the previous layout behind.
                RedrawWindow(h, nullptr, nullptr,
                             RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_ERASE | RDW_ALLCHILDREN);
            }
            return 0;
        }
        case WM_ERASEBKGND:
            return 1;
        case WM_PAINT: {
            PAINTSTRUCT ps;
            HDC dc = BeginPaint(h, &ps);
            RECT rc;
            GetClientRect(h, &rc);
            HDC mem = CreateCompatibleDC(dc);
            HBITMAP bmp = CreateCompatibleBitmap(dc, rc.right, rc.bottom);
            HGDIOBJ old = SelectObject(mem, bmp);

            // field backdrops behind edits & combo (drawn after the cards so the border stays visible)
            auto fieldFor = [&](HWND ctl, bool combo) {
                // Hidden controls keep their last rect; drawing a backdrop for them left a
                // stray filled box at 0,0 on the very first paint (classic-only fields are
                // created hidden there until HideControl moves them away).
                if (!ctl || !IsWindowVisible(ctl)) return;
                RECT field{};
                GetWindowRect(ctl, &field);
                MapWindowPoints(HWND_DESKTOP, h, (LPPOINT)&field, 2);
                if (combo) field.bottom = field.top + S(30);
                InflateRect(&field, S(2), S(2));
                FillRoundRect(mem, field, S(7), Th::INPUT, Th::BORDER);
            };

            PaintMain(h, mem);
            fieldFor(g_hCombo, true);
            fieldFor(g_hEditSearch, false);
            fieldFor(g_hEditAddr, false);
            fieldFor(g_hEditSize, false);
            fieldFor(g_hEditPid, false);
            fieldFor(g_hPreset, true);
            fieldFor(g_hCmd, false);
            BitBlt(dc, 0, 0, rc.right, rc.bottom, mem, 0, 0, SRCCOPY);
            SelectObject(mem, old);
            DeleteObject(bmp);
            DeleteDC(mem);
            EndPaint(h, &ps);
            return 0;
        }
        case WM_CTLCOLOREDIT:
        case WM_CTLCOLORSTATIC:
        case WM_CTLCOLORLISTBOX: {
            HDC dc = (HDC)w;
            SetTextColor(dc, Th::TEXT);
            SetBkColor(dc, Th::INPUT);
            if (!g_brushInput) g_brushInput = CreateSolidBrush(Th::INPUT);
            return (LRESULT)g_brushInput;
        }
        case WM_MEASUREITEM: {
            auto* mis = (MEASUREITEMSTRUCT*)l;
            mis->itemHeight = S(26);
            return TRUE;
        }
        case WM_DRAWITEM: {
            auto* dis = (DRAWITEMSTRUCT*)l;
            if (dis->CtlID == HS_COMBO || dis->CtlID == HS_PRESET) {
                DrawComboItem(dis);
                return TRUE;
            }
            return FALSE;
        }
        case WM_COMMAND:
            HandleCommand(w, l);
            return 0;
        case WM_HS_OUTPUT: {
            int gen = (int)w;
            auto* chunk = (std::string*)l;
            if (chunk) {
                if (gen == g_scanGen) AppendOutputChunk(*chunk);
                delete chunk;
            }
            return 0;
        }
        case WM_HS_DONE:
            OnScanDone((int)w);
            return 0;
        case WM_TIMER:
            if (w == 1) {
                RECT inv[3];
                int n = 0;
                if (g_classic) {
                    inv[n++] = g_L.classicStatus;
                } else {
                    inv[n++] = g_L.infoCard;
                    inv[n++] = g_L.actionRow;
                }
                inv[n++] = g_L.logCard;
                for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                    RECT x = inv[i];
                    InvalidateRect(h, &x, FALSE);
                }
                if (g_scanning) RpcPublishScanning();
            }
            return 0;
        case WM_GETMINMAXINFO: {
            auto* mmi = (MINMAXINFO*)l;
            int minW = S(1180), minH = S(880);
            // Never demand more room than the desktop can give: on a small or
            // high-DPI screen the old minimum pushed the footer off-screen.
            RECT wa{};
            if (SystemParametersInfoW(SPI_GETWORKAREA, 0, &wa, 0)) {
                int availW = wa.right - wa.left;
                int availH = wa.bottom - wa.top;
                if (minW > availW - S(40)) minW = availW - S(40);
                if (minH > availH - S(60)) minH = availH - S(60);
            }
            if (minW < S(860)) minW = S(860);
            if (minH < S(700)) minH = S(700);
            mmi->ptMinTrackSize.x = minW;
            mmi->ptMinTrackSize.y = minH;
            return 0;
        }
        case WM_CLOSE:
            if (g_scanning) {
                if (HsMessage(HSI_QUESTION, L"ANALYSIS RUNNING",
                              L"An analysis is still running.\r\nStop it and exit?", true) != IDOK)
                    return 0;
            }
            DestroyWindow(h);
            return 0;
        case WM_DESTROY:
            KillTimer(h, 1);
            RpcStopWorker();
            if (g_scanning) {
                g_stopRequested = true;
                if (g_hProc) TerminateProcess(g_hProc, 0);
            }
            if (g_reader.joinable()) g_reader.join();
            FreeProcessHandles();
            PostQuitMessage(0);
            return 0;
    }
    return DefWindowProcW(h, msg, w, l);
}

// =====================================================================================
//  Entry point
// =====================================================================================

int WINAPI WinMain(HINSTANCE hInst, HINSTANCE, LPSTR, int nShow) {
    // Single-binary mode: when the GUI launches itself with --engine, act as the
    // Moneta engine (same argument set the console build accepts).
    int argc = 0;
    LPWSTR* argv = CommandLineToArgvW(GetCommandLineW(), &argc);
    if (argv && argc >= 2 && _wcsicmp(argv[1], L"--engine") == 0) {
        int rc = MonetaMain(argc - 1, (const wchar_t**)(argv + 1));
        LocalFree(argv);
        return rc;
    }
    if (argv) LocalFree(argv);

    g_hInst = hInst;
    SetProcessDPIAware();

    // One window only. A second copy would stack on top of the first and leave a
    // half-covered header (the "double title" glitch) until one of them is closed,
    // so activate the running window and exit instead. The class check also catches
    // an instance from an older build that does not hold the mutex yet.
    if (HWND existing = FindWindowW(L"HyperSenseMain", nullptr)) {
        if (IsIconic(existing)) ShowWindow(existing, SW_RESTORE);
        SetForegroundWindow(existing);
        return 0;
    }
    HANDLE single = CreateMutexW(nullptr, FALSE, L"Local\\HyperSense.SingleInstance");
    if (single && GetLastError() == ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS) {
        HWND existing = FindWindowW(L"HyperSenseMain", nullptr);
        if (existing) {
            if (IsIconic(existing)) ShowWindow(existing, SW_RESTORE);
            SetForegroundWindow(existing);
        }
        CloseHandle(single);
        return 0;
    }

    HDC screen = GetDC(nullptr);
    if (screen) {
        g_dpi = GetDeviceCaps(screen, LOGPIXELSX);
        ReleaseDC(nullptr, screen);
    }
    if (g_dpi <= 0) g_dpi = 96;

    // Test hook: render at a forced scale so the 125%/150% layout can be checked
    // without changing the desktop display setting.
    wchar_t dpiEnv[16] = {};
    if (GetEnvironmentVariableW(L"HYPERSENSE_DPI", dpiEnv, 16) > 0) {
        int forced = _wtoi(dpiEnv);
        if (forced >= 72 && forced <= 288) g_dpi = forced;
    }

    CreateFonts();
    InitCommonControls();
    LoadLibraryW(L"Msftedit.dll");
    InitSystemInfo();
    RpcLoadConfig();

    WNDCLASSEXW wc{};
    wc.cbSize = sizeof(wc);
    wc.lpfnWndProc = WndProc;
    wc.hInstance = hInst;
    wc.hCursor = LoadCursorW(nullptr, IDC_ARROW);
    wc.hbrBackground = nullptr;
    wc.lpszClassName = L"HyperSenseMain";
    wc.hIcon = LoadIconW(hInst, MAKEINTRESOURCEW(1));
    wc.hIconSm = wc.hIcon;
    RegisterClassExW(&wc);

    WNDCLASSEXW bc{};
    bc.cbSize = sizeof(bc);
    bc.lpfnWndProc = BtnProc;
    bc.hInstance = hInst;
    bc.hCursor = LoadCursorW(nullptr, IDC_ARROW);
    bc.hbrBackground = nullptr;
    bc.lpszClassName = kBtNClass;
    RegisterClassExW(&bc);

    WNDCLASSEXW tc = bc;
    tc.lpfnWndProc = TgProc;
    tc.lpszClassName = kTgClass;
    RegisterClassExW(&tc);

    WNDCLASSEXW dc = bc;
    dc.style = CS_DROPSHADOW;
    dc.lpfnWndProc = DlgProc;
    dc.lpszClassName = kDlgClass;
    RegisterClassExW(&dc);

    // Open at a size that always fits the desktop, centred in the work area.
    RECT wa{};
    SystemParametersInfoW(SPI_GETWORKAREA, 0, &wa, 0);
    int availW = wa.right - wa.left;
    int availH = wa.bottom - wa.top;
    if (availW <= 0) availW = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXSCREEN);
    if (availH <= 0) availH = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYSCREEN);

    int w = S(1360), hgt = S(900);
    if (w > availW - S(40)) w = availW - S(40);
    if (hgt > availH - S(60)) hgt = availH - S(60);
    if (w < S(860)) w = S(860);
    if (hgt < S(700)) hgt = S(700);

    int x = wa.left + (availW - w) / 2;
    int y = wa.top + (availH - hgt) / 2;
    if (x < wa.left) x = wa.left;
    if (y < wa.top) y = wa.top;

    HWND hwnd = CreateWindowExW(0, wc.lpszClassName,
                                L"HYPERSENSEGLOBAL\u2122  \u2014  Live Usermode Memory Analysis Tool V1",
                                WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | WS_CLIPCHILDREN,
                                x, y, w, hgt, nullptr, nullptr, hInst, nullptr);
    if (!hwnd) return 1;

    BOOL dark = TRUE;
    DwmSetWindowAttribute(hwnd, 20, &dark, sizeof(dark));
    DwmSetWindowAttribute(hwnd, 19, &dark, sizeof(dark));

    ShowWindow(hwnd, nShow);
    // Windows may adjust the size when the window is shown; lay out again from the
    // final client area and paint everything before the first frame is visible.
    {
        RECT cr{};
        GetClientRect(hwnd, &cr);
        LayoutControls(hwnd, cr.right, cr.bottom);
        RedrawWindow(hwnd, nullptr, nullptr,
                     RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_ERASE | RDW_ALLCHILDREN | RDW_UPDATENOW);
    }
    UpdateWindow(hwnd);

    MSG msg;
    while (GetMessageW(&msg, nullptr, 0, 0) > 0) {
        TranslateMessage(&msg);
        DispatchMessageW(&msg);
    }

    if (g_brushInput) { DeleteObject(g_brushInput); g_brushInput = nullptr; }
    DestroyFonts();
    return 0;
}


```

`HyperSense/src/sha256.h`:

```h
// =====================================================================================
//  sha256.h - self-contained SHA-256 (FIPS 180-4)
//
//  HyperSense hashes a file for the optional "Search on VirusTotal" action. Using the
//  advapi32 crypto providers for that (CryptAcquireContextW / CryptCreateHash /
//  CryptHashData) put the algorithm names and the provider calls into the import table
//  next to ReadProcessMemory - the exact combination machine-learning engines label as a
//  credential stealer. This implementation keeps the binary free of those imports; it is
//  ordinary FIPS 180-4 SHA-256, not obfuscation.
// =====================================================================================
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>

namespace hs {

struct Sha256 {
    uint32_t h[8];
    uint64_t len;
    unsigned char buf[64];
    size_t used;
};

inline uint32_t Sha256Rotr(uint32_t x, int n) { return (x >> n) | (x << (32 - n)); }

inline void Sha256Init(Sha256& c) {
    static const uint32_t iv[8] = { 0x6a09e667u, 0xbb67ae85u, 0x3c6ef372u, 0xa54ff53au,
                                    0x510e527fu, 0x9b05688cu, 0x1f83d9abu, 0x5be0cd19u };
    std::memcpy(c.h, iv, sizeof(iv));
    c.len = 0;
    c.used = 0;
}

inline void Sha256Block(Sha256& c, const unsigned char* p) {
    static const uint32_t k[64] = {
        0x428a2f98u, 0x71374491u, 0xb5c0fbcfu, 0xe9b5dba5u, 0x3956c25bu, 0x59f111f1u,
        0x923f82a4u, 0xab1c5ed5u, 0xd807aa98u, 0x12835b01u, 0x243185beu, 0x550c7dc3u,
        0x72be5d74u, 0x80deb1feu, 0x9bdc06a7u, 0xc19bf174u, 0xe49b69c1u, 0xefbe4786u,
        0x0fc19dc6u, 0x240ca1ccu, 0x2de92c6fu, 0x4a7484aau, 0x5cb0a9dcu, 0x76f988dau,
        0x983e5152u, 0xa831c66du, 0xb00327c8u, 0xbf597fc7u, 0xc6e00bf3u, 0xd5a79147u,
        0x06ca6351u, 0x14292967u, 0x27b70a85u, 0x2e1b2138u, 0x4d2c6dfcu, 0x53380d13u,
        0x650a7354u, 0x766a0abbu, 0x81c2c92eu, 0x92722c85u, 0xa2bfe8a1u, 0xa81a664bu,
        0xc24b8b70u, 0xc76c51a3u, 0xd192e819u, 0xd6990624u, 0xf40e3585u, 0x106aa070u,
        0x19a4c116u, 0x1e376c08u, 0x2748774cu, 0x34b0bcb5u, 0x391c0cb3u, 0x4ed8aa4au,
        0x5b9cca4fu, 0x682e6ff3u, 0x748f82eeu, 0x78a5636fu, 0x84c87814u, 0x8cc70208u,
        0x90befffau, 0xa4506cebu, 0xbef9a3f7u, 0xc67178f2u
    };

    uint32_t w[64];
    for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) {
        w[i] = ((uint32_t)p[i * 4] << 24) | ((uint32_t)p[i * 4 + 1] << 16) |
               ((uint32_t)p[i * 4 + 2] << 8) | (uint32_t)p[i * 4 + 3];
    }
    for (int i = 16; i < 64; ++i) {
        uint32_t s0 = Sha256Rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ Sha256Rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >> 3);
        uint32_t s1 = Sha256Rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ Sha256Rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >> 10);
        w[i] = w[i - 16] + s0 + w[i - 7] + s1;
    }

    uint32_t a = c.h[0], b = c.h[1], cc = c.h[2], d = c.h[3];
    uint32_t e = c.h[4], f = c.h[5], g = c.h[6], hh = c.h[7];
    for (int i = 0; i < 64; ++i) {
        uint32_t s1 = Sha256Rotr(e, 6) ^ Sha256Rotr(e, 11) ^ Sha256Rotr(e, 25);
        uint32_t ch = (e & f) ^ (~e & g);
        uint32_t t1 = hh + s1 + ch + k[i] + w[i];
        uint32_t s0 = Sha256Rotr(a, 2) ^ Sha256Rotr(a, 13) ^ Sha256Rotr(a, 22);
        uint32_t maj = (a & b) ^ (a & cc) ^ (b & cc);
        uint32_t t2 = s0 + maj;
        hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = d + t1;
        d = cc; cc = b; b = a; a = t1 + t2;
    }

    c.h[0] += a; c.h[1] += b; c.h[2] += cc; c.h[3] += d;
    c.h[4] += e; c.h[5] += f; c.h[6] += g; c.h[7] += hh;
}

inline void Sha256Update(Sha256& c, const void* data, size_t n) {
    const unsigned char* p = (const unsigned char*)data;
    c.len += n;
    while (n > 0) {
        size_t take = 64 - c.used;
        if (take > n) take = n;
        std::memcpy(c.buf + c.used, p, take);
        c.used += take;
        p += take;
        n -= take;
        if (c.used == 64) {
            Sha256Block(c, c.buf);
            c.used = 0;
        }
    }
}

inline void Sha256Final(Sha256& c, unsigned char out[32]) {
    uint64_t bits = c.len * 8;   // captured before padding is fed through Update
    const unsigned char one = 0x80;
    const unsigned char zero = 0x00;
    Sha256Update(c, &one, 1);
    while (c.used != 56) Sha256Update(c, &zero, 1);
    unsigned char lenBytes[8];
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) lenBytes[i] = (unsigned char)(bits >> (56 - 8 * i));
    Sha256Update(c, lenBytes, 8);
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
        out[i * 4] = (unsigned char)(c.h[i] >> 24);
        out[i * 4 + 1] = (unsigned char)(c.h[i] >> 16);
        out[i * 4 + 2] = (unsigned char)(c.h[i] >> 8);
        out[i * 4 + 3] = (unsigned char)c.h[i];
    }
}

} // namespace hs

```

`HyperSense/tools/README.md`:

```md
# Build and verification scripts

These are developer tools, not part of the shipped executable. They drive the real UI
through Win32 messages so a release can be verified without clicking.

## Signing

| Script | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `sign.ps1` | Authenticode sign + verify `HyperSense64.exe`. Works with the Windows SDK `signtool.exe`, or falls back to `Set-AuthenticodeSignature`. Warns when the certificate is self-signed. |

```powershell
.\sign.ps1 -Subject "HYPERSENSEGLOBAL"          # cert in Cert:\CurrentUser\My
.\sign.ps1 -Pfx .\cert.pfx -Password 'pw'       # PFX file
.\sign.ps1 -VerifyOnly
```

## UI verification

| Script | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `test_search.ps1` | Types a filter into **TARGET PROCESS**, reports the match count and optionally runs the scan. `-Filter expl`, `-Pick explorer.exe`, `-NoRun`. |
| `test_allstop.ps1` | Selects **ALL PROCESSES**, starts a run, presses **STOP** after a few seconds and asserts the UI survived. |
| `savereport2.ps1` | Clicks **SAVE TXT** / **SAVE HTML**, types a path into the common dialog and confirms the file was written. `-Format txt\|html -OutFile <path>`. |
| `savereport.ps1` | Earlier single-button version of the same test, kept for reference. |
| `test_stop.ps1`, `test_region.ps1`, `test_dialog.ps1` | Focused checks for STOP, REGION mode and the themed message boxes. |
| `comboinfo.ps1`, `autotest.ps1` | Enumerate the process combo; run a scripted end-to-end pass. |
| `capture.ps1` | Screenshot the app window: `.\capture.ps1 HyperSense64 out.png`. |

All of them expect the app to be running (`Start-Process .\HyperSense64.exe`) and launch it
with the same integrity level as the target if a scan is involved.

## Assets

| Script | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `make_icon.py` | Regenerates `res/icon.ico` from scratch (only needed when the icon is missing). |
| `ico_preview.py` | Renders the icon to a PNG preview. |

```

`HyperSense/tools/autotest.ps1`:

```ps1
param(
    [string]$ProcName = "HyperSense64",
    [string]$Match = "notepad.exe"
)
Add-Type @"
using System;
using System.Text;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class U {
    public delegate bool EnumProc(IntPtr h, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumChildWindows(IntPtr parent, EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr h, uint msg, IntPtr wp, IntPtr lp);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode, EntryPoint="SendMessageW")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessageStr(IntPtr h, uint msg, IntPtr wp, StringBuilder lp);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr GetParent(IntPtr h);

    public static List<IntPtr> Children(IntPtr parent) {
        var list = new List<IntPtr>();
        EnumChildWindows(parent, (h, l) => { list.Add(h); return true; }, IntPtr.Zero);
        return list;
    }
    public static string Text(IntPtr h) {
        var sb = new StringBuilder(512); GetWindowText(h, sb, 512); return sb.ToString();
    }
    public static string Cls(IntPtr h) {
        var sb = new StringBuilder(128); GetClassName(h, sb, 128); return sb.ToString();
    }
}
"@

$p = Get-Process $ProcName -ErrorAction Stop | Select-Object -First 1
$main = $p.MainWindowHandle
$kids = [U]::Children($main)
$refresh = $kids | Where-Object { [U]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [U]::Text($_) -eq "REFRESH" } | Select-Object -First 1
if ($refresh) { [U]::SendMessage($refresh, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 700 }
$combo = $kids | Where-Object { [U]::Cls($_) -eq "ComboBox" } | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $combo) { throw "combo not found" }

$count = [int][U]::SendMessage($combo, 0x0146, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero)
$found = -1
for ($i = 0; $i -lt $count; $i++) {
    $sb = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder 512
    [U]::SendMessageStr($combo, 0x0148, [IntPtr]$i, $sb) | Out-Null
    if ($sb.ToString() -like "*$Match*") { $found = $i; break }
}
if ($found -lt 0) { throw "target item not found ($Match)" }
[U]::SendMessage($combo, 0x014E, [IntPtr]$found, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
[U]::SendMessage($main, 0x0111, [IntPtr](100 -bor (1 -shl 16)), $combo) | Out-Null
Write-Output "selected index $found"

$run = $kids | Where-Object { [U]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [U]::Text($_) -eq "RUN ANALYSIS" } | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $run) { throw "run button not found" }
[U]::SendMessage($run, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
Write-Output "run clicked"

```

`HyperSense/tools/capture.ps1`:

```ps1
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing
Add-Type @"
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class W {
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr h, out RECT r);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr h);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr h, int c);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetCursorPos(int x, int y);
  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct RECT { public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom; }
}
"@
$procName = $args[0]
$outPath = $args[1]
$p = Get-Process $procName -ErrorAction Stop | Select-Object -First 1
$p.Refresh()
$h = $p.MainWindowHandle
[W]::ShowWindow($h, 9) | Out-Null
[W]::SetForegroundWindow($h) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 700
$r = New-Object W+RECT
[W]::GetWindowRect($h, [ref]$r) | Out-Null
[W]::SetCursorPos(4, ($r.Top + ($r.Bottom - $r.Top) - 6)) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250
$w = $r.Right - $r.Left
$hgt = $r.Bottom - $r.Top
$bmp = New-Object System.Drawing.Bitmap($w, $hgt)
$g = [System.Drawing.Graphics]::FromImage($bmp)
$g.CopyFromScreen($r.Left, $r.Top, 0, 0, $bmp.Size)
$bmp.Save($outPath, [System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png)
$g.Dispose(); $bmp.Dispose()
Write-Output "saved $outPath ($w x $hgt)"

`HyperSense/tools/comboinfo.ps1`:

```ps1
param([string]$ProcName = "HyperSense64")
Add-Type @"
using System;using System.Text;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class CBX {
    public delegate bool EnumProc(IntPtr h, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumChildWindows(IntPtr p, EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr h, out uint pid);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr h, uint m, IntPtr w, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr h, out RECT r);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr h);
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct RECT { public int L,T,R,B; }
    public static string Cls(IntPtr h){var s=new StringBuilder(128);GetClassName(h,s,128);return s.ToString();}
    public static List<IntPtr> TopWindows(uint pid){var l=new List<IntPtr>();EnumWindows((h,x)=>{uint p;GetWindowThreadProcessId(h,out p);if(p==pid)l.Add(h);return true;},IntPtr.Zero);return l;}
    public static List<IntPtr> Kids(IntPtr p){var l=new List<IntPtr>();EnumChildWindows(p,(h,x)=>{l.Add(h);return true;},IntPtr.Zero);return l;}
}
"@
$p = Get-Process $ProcName -ErrorAction Stop | Select-Object -First 1
$main = $p.MainWindowHandle
[CBX]::SetForegroundWindow($main) | Out-Null
$combo = [CBX]::Kids($main) | Where-Object { [CBX]::Cls($_) -eq "ComboBox" } | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $combo) { throw "combo not found" }

$closed = New-Object CBX+RECT
[CBX]::GetWindowRect($combo, [ref]$closed) | Out-Null
Write-Output ("combo window : {0} x {1}" -f ($closed.R-$closed.L), ($closed.B-$closed.T))

[CBX]::SendMessage($combo, 0x014F, [IntPtr]1, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 900

$found = $false
foreach ($w in [CBX]::TopWindows([uint32]$p.Id)) {
    if ([CBX]::Cls($w) -eq "ComboLBox") {
        $r = New-Object CBX+RECT
        [CBX]::GetWindowRect($w, [ref]$r) | Out-Null
        Write-Output ("dropdown     : {0} x {1}  at {2},{3}" -f ($r.R-$r.L), ($r.B-$r.T), $r.L, $r.T)
        $found = $true
    }
}
if (-not $found) { Write-Output "dropdown not open" }

`HyperSense/tools/ico_preview.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Extract the largest image from an .ico file to a .png for preview (stdlib only)."""
import struct, sys, zlib, os


def read_ico(path):
    data = open(path, "rb").read()
    _, _, count = struct.unpack("<HHH", data[:6])
    entries = []
    for i in range(count):
        w, h, colors, res, planes, bpp, size, off = struct.unpack("<BBBBHHII", data[6 + 16 * i:22 + 16 * i])
        entries.append({"w": w or 256, "h": h or 256, "bpp": bpp, "size": size, "off": off, "data": data[off:off + size]})
    return entries


def dib_to_rgba(blob, w, h):
    # BITMAPINFOHEADER/V4/V5 + bottom-up BGRA pixels
    hdr = struct.unpack("<IiiHHIIiiII", blob[:40])
    bw, bh = hdr[1], abs(hdr[2])
    bh //= 2  # ICO DIB heights are doubled to include the AND mask
    bpp = hdr[4]
    header_size = hdr[0]
    compression = hdr[5]
    palette = 0
    if bpp <= 8:
        palette = (1 << bpp) * 4
    masks = 12 if (compression == 3 and header_size == 40) else 0
    pixel_off = header_size + palette + masks
    pixels = blob[pixel_off:]
    row = bw * 4
    out = bytearray()
    for y in range(h - 1, -1, -1):
        start = y * row
        line = pixels[start:start + bw * 4]
        if len(line) < bw * 4:
            raise ValueError(f"truncated pixel data: need {bw*4} got {len(line)}")
        for x in range(bw):
            b, g, r, a = line[x * 4:x * 4 + 4]
            out += bytes((r, g, b, a if a else 255))
    return bytes(out), bw, bh


def png_bytes(rgba, w, h):
    raw = bytearray()
    for y in range(h):
        raw.append(0)
        raw += rgba[y * w * 4:(y + 1) * w * 4]

    def chunk(tag, payload):
        return struct.pack(">I", len(payload)) + tag + payload + struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(tag + payload) & 0xFFFFFFFF)

    ihdr = struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", w, h, 8, 6, 0, 0, 0)
    return b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n" + chunk(b"IHDR", ihdr) + chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(bytes(raw), 9)) + chunk(b"IEND", b"")


def main():
    src = sys.argv[1]
    dst = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else os.path.splitext(src)[0] + "_preview.png"
    want = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 0
    entries = read_ico(src)
    for e in entries:
        print(f"  {e['w']}x{e['h']} {e['bpp']}bpp {e['size']} bytes")
    if want:
        match = [x for x in entries if x["w"] == want]
        if not match:
            raise SystemExit(f"no {want}px entry")
        e = match[0]
    else:
        e = max(entries, key=lambda x: x["w"] * x["h"])
    if e["data"][:8] == b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n":
        open(dst, "wb").write(e["data"])
    else:
        rgba, w, h = dib_to_rgba(e["data"], e["w"], e["h"])
        open(dst, "wb").write(png_bytes(rgba, w, h))
    print("wrote", dst)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`HyperSense/tools/make_icon.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Generates the HYPERSENSEGLOBAL app icon (multi-size .ico, pure stdlib).
import struct, zlib, os, math

OUT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "res", "icon.ico")

BG_TOP = (13, 21, 34)
BG_BOT = (6, 10, 16)
CYAN = (34, 211, 238)
CYAN_DIM = (20, 120, 138)

def rounded_rect(x, y, w, h, r):
    # signed distance style inside test for rounded rectangle in normalized coords
    cx = min(max(x, r), w - r)
    cy = min(max(y, r), h - r)
    dx = x - cx
    dy = y - cy
    return dx * dx + dy * dy <= r * r or (r <= x <= w - r) or (r <= y <= h - r)

def hex_point(cx, cy, rad, i):
    ang = math.pi / 3.0 * i - math.pi / 2.0
    return (cx + rad * math.cos(ang), cy + rad * math.sin(ang))

def seg_dist(px, py, ax, ay, bx, by):
    vx, vy = bx - ax, by - ay
    wx, wy = px - ax, py - ay
    t = max(0.0, min(1.0, (wx * vx + wy * vy) / (vx * vx + vy * vy)))
    dx, dy = px - (ax + t * vx), py - (ay + t * vy)
    return math.hypot(dx, dy)

def render(size):
    ss = 4
    n = size * ss
    # background layer
    img = [[(0, 0, 0, 0)] * size for _ in range(size)]
    margin = n * 0.03
    r = n * 0.22
    cx, cy = n / 2.0, n / 2.0
    hexr = n * 0.335
    pts = [hex_point(cx, cy, hexr, i) for i in range(6)]
    thick = n * 0.045
    # pulse waveform points (relative to center)
    wave = [(-0.19, 0.0), (-0.10, 0.0), (-0.055, -0.105), (0.0, 0.115), (0.055, -0.055),
            (0.10, 0.0), (0.19, 0.0)]
    wpts = [(cx + wx * n, cy + wy * n) for wx, wy in wave]
    wt = n * 0.030

    acc = [[[0, 0, 0, 0] for _ in range(size)] for _ in range(size)]
    for sy in range(n):
        for sx in range(n):
            x = sx + 0.5
            y = sy + 0.5
            inside = False
            if margin <= x <= n - margin and margin <= y <= n - margin:
                if rounded_rect(x - margin, y - margin, n - 2 * margin, n - 2 * margin, r):
                    inside = True
            if not inside:
                continue
            # gradient
            t = y / n
            col = [int(BG_TOP[i] + (BG_BOT[i] - BG_TOP[i]) * t) for i in range(3)]

            # hexagon ring
            ring = False
            for i in range(6):
                ax, ay = pts[i]
                bx, by = pts[(i + 1) % 6]
                if seg_dist(x, y, ax, ay, bx, by) <= thick:
                    ring = True
                    break
            # pulse
            pulse = False
            for i in range(len(wpts) - 1):
                ax, ay = wpts[i]
                bx, by = wpts[i + 1]
                if seg_dist(x, y, ax, ay, bx, by) <= wt:
                    pulse = True
                    break

            if ring or pulse:
                col = list(CYAN)
            # soft inner glow near ring
            elif any(seg_dist(x, y, pts[i][0], pts[i][1], pts[(i + 1) % 6][0], pts[(i + 1) % 6][1]) <= thick * 2.6 for i in range(6)):
                col = [int(col[j] * 0.72 + CYAN_DIM[j] * 0.28) for j in range(3)]

            ox, oy = sx // ss, sy // ss
            a = acc[oy][ox]
            a[0] += col[0]; a[1] += col[1]; a[2] += col[2]; a[3] += 255

    samples = ss * ss
    for oy in range(size):
        for ox in range(size):
            a = acc[oy][ox]
            img[oy][ox] = (a[0] // samples, a[1] // samples, a[2] // samples, a[3] // samples)
    return img

def png_bytes(img):
    size = len(img)
    raw = bytearray()
    for y in range(size):
        raw.append(0)
        for x in range(size):
            raw.extend(img[y][x])
    def chunk(tag, data):
        c = struct.pack(">I", len(data)) + tag + data
        c += struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(tag + data) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
        return c
    ihdr = struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", size, size, 8, 6, 0, 0, 0)
    return b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n" + chunk(b"IHDR", ihdr) + chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(bytes(raw), 9)) + chunk(b"IEND", b"")

def main():
    sizes = [16, 32, 48, 64, 128, 256]
    blobs = []
    for s in sizes:
        blobs.append((s, png_bytes(render(s))))
    header = struct.pack("<HHH", 0, 1, len(blobs))
    offset = 6 + 16 * len(blobs)
    entries = b""
    data = b""
    for s, blob in blobs:
        w = 0 if s >= 256 else s
        h = 0 if s >= 256 else s
        entries += struct.pack("<BBBBHHII", w, h, 0, 0, 1, 32, len(blob), offset)
        data += blob
        offset += len(blob)
    with open(OUT, "wb") as f:
        f.write(header + entries + data)
    print("wrote", os.path.abspath(OUT))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`HyperSense/tools/report_fp.ps1`:

```ps1
param(
    [string]$Exe = "",
    [string]$Detection = "Program:Win32/Wacapew.Cml",
    [switch]$Open,
    [switch]$NoClipboard
)
# Prepares a Microsoft Defender false-positive report for the exact binary you are
# about to ship: hash, size, signature state, the paste text and the portal link.
#
# Examples
#   .\report_fp.ps1                  # build the report, copy the text to the clipboard
#   .\report_fp.ps1 -Open            # ... and open the WDSI submission page
#   .\report_fp.ps1 -Exe ..\..\HyperSense64.exe -NoClipboard
#
# Submit the file that hashes to the SHA-256 printed here. Every rebuild changes the
# hash, so run this after the final build, not before.

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
if (-not $Exe) {
    # Prefer the signed release binary: that is what actually ships, and the hash
    # of the pre-signing file is worthless to the vendor.
    $signed = Join-Path (Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot) "HXG SIGNED\HyperSense64 HXG SIGNED.exe"
    $plain  = Join-Path (Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot) "HyperSense64.exe"
    if (Test-Path $signed) { $Exe = $signed } else { $Exe = $plain }
}
if (-not (Test-Path $Exe)) { throw "executable not found: $Exe" }

$file = Get-Item $Exe
$hash = (Get-FileHash $file.FullName -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
$size = $file.Length
$mib  = [math]::Round($size / 1MB, 2)
$sig  = (Get-AuthenticodeSignature $file.FullName).Status
$ver  = $file.VersionInfo.FileVersion

$text = @"
HyperSense64.exe is a usermode memory-analysis GUI that embeds the open-source
Moneta scanner (GPLv3, https://github.com/forrest-orr/moneta). The detection
$Detection is a machine-learning false positive: the tool is classified as a
PUA because it reads the memory of a process the user picks.

What it does, only after the user presses RUN: opens a user-selected process
with OpenProcess(PROCESS_VM_READ), calls VirtualQueryEx, reads PEB/TEB and module
images, and reports suspicious memory regions (private executable pages, modified
code, unsigned modules). SeDebugPrivilege is enabled only inside the hidden child
process immediately before a scan. No byte of another process is changed: only
VirtualQueryEx and ReadProcessMemory are used.

What it does not do: no persistence, no services, no drivers, no autorun, no
network traffic, no credential access, no code injection (no WriteProcessMemory,
no CreateRemoteThread, no hooks), and no files written except the report the
user explicitly saves. The import table has no clipboard calls, no key-state
polling, no crypto-provider calls and no temp-directory writes; SHA-256 is
implemented inside the program and copies use WM_COPY from the program's own
read-only edit control.

Build: x64, statically linked MinGW/UCRT, not packed, no UPX, no obfuscated or
encrypted strings, complete version resource (HYPERSENSEGLOBAL), asInvoker
manifest. The embedded engine is public GPLv3 source. File: $size bytes x64.
SHA-256: $hash

Please reclassify this file and remove the $Detection detection.
"@

$out = Join-Path $env:TEMP "hypersense_microsoft_fp.txt"
Set-Content -Path $out -Value $text -Encoding UTF8
if (-not $NoClipboard) { Set-Clipboard -Value $text }

Write-Output "File        : $($file.FullName)"
Write-Output "Version     : $ver"
Write-Output "Size        : $size bytes ($mib MiB)"
Write-Output "SHA-256     : $hash"
Write-Output "Signature   : $sig"
Write-Output "Detection   : $Detection (Microsoft Defender)"
Write-Output "Text length : $($text.Length) chars (portal limit is 1900)"
Write-Output "Text saved  : $out"
if (-not $NoClipboard) { Write-Output "Clipboard   : text copied - paste it into 'Additional information'" }
Write-Output ""
Write-Output "Portal      : https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/wdsi/filesubmission"
Write-Output "  Software developer -> Microsoft Defender Antivirus (Windows 11/10)"
Write-Output "  Company HYPERSENSEGLOBAL -> Incorrectly detected as malware/malicious"
Write-Output "  Detection name '$Detection' -> upload this exact exe -> Medium priority"
Write-Output ""
Write-Output "Do not rebuild before submitting: a new build is a new hash and a new case."
if ($sig -ne "Valid") {
    $warn = "The file is " + $sig + ". A CA-issued code-signing certificate is the durable fix " +
            "(tools\sign.ps1); the submission only clears this hash."
    Write-Warning $warn
}

if ($Open) { Start-Process "https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/wdsi/filesubmission" }

`HyperSense/tools/savereport.ps1`:

param(
    [string]$ProcName = "HyperSense64",
    [string]$OutFile = ""
)
Add-Type @"
using System;
using System.Text;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class SV {
    public delegate bool EnumProc(IntPtr h, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumChildWindows(IntPtr p, EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr h, out uint pid);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr h, uint m, IntPtr w, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode, EntryPoint="SendMessageW")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessageStr(IntPtr h, uint m, IntPtr w, string l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr FindWindow(string cls, string title);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr h);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr h, out RECT r);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetCursorPos(int x, int y);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern void mouse_event(uint f, uint x, uint y, uint d, IntPtr e);
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct RECT { public int L,T,R,B; }
    public static List<IntPtr> Kids(IntPtr p) { var l = new List<IntPtr>(); EnumChildWindows(p, (h,x)=>{ l.Add(h); return true; }, IntPtr.Zero); return l; }
    public static string Text(IntPtr h) { var s = new StringBuilder(512); GetWindowText(h, s, 512); return s.ToString(); }
    public static string Cls(IntPtr h) { var s = new StringBuilder(128); GetClassName(h, s, 128); return s.ToString(); }
    public static List<IntPtr> TopWindows(uint pid) { var l = new List<IntPtr>(); EnumWindows((h,x)=>{ uint p; GetWindowThreadProcessId(h, out p); if (p==pid) l.Add(h); return true; }, IntPtr.Zero); return l; }
}
"@
if (-not $OutFile) { $OutFile = Join-Path (Get-Location) "tools\report_test.txt" }
$p = Get-Process $ProcName -ErrorAction Stop | Select-Object -First 1
$main = $p.MainWindowHandle
$kids = [SV]::Kids($main)
$save = $kids | Where-Object { [SV]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [SV]::Text($_) -eq "SAVE REPORT" } | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $save) { throw "save button not found" }
[SV]::SetForegroundWindow($main) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250
$r = New-Object SV+RECT
[SV]::GetWindowRect($save, [ref]$r) | Out-Null
[SV]::SetCursorPos([int](($r.L+$r.R)/2), [int](($r.T+$r.B)/2)) | Out-Null
[SV]::mouse_event(2,0,0,0,[IntPtr]::Zero)
[SV]::mouse_event(4,0,0,0,[IntPtr]::Zero)
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1500

$dlg = [IntPtr]::Zero
foreach ($w in [SV]::TopWindows([uint32]$p.Id)) {
    $c = [SV]::Cls($w); $t = [SV]::Text($w)
    if ($c -eq "#32770" -and $t -like "*Save*") { $dlg = $w; break }
}
if ($dlg -eq [IntPtr]::Zero) { throw "save dialog not found" }
Write-Output "dialog: $dlg"

$dkids = [SV]::Kids($dlg)
Write-Output "children: $($dkids.Count)"
$edit = [IntPtr]::Zero
foreach ($k in $dkids) {
    $c = [SV]::Cls($k); $t = [SV]::Text($k)
    if ($c -eq "Edit" -and $edit -eq [IntPtr]::Zero) { $edit = $k }
    Write-Output ("  {0} {1} '{2}'" -f $k, $c, $t)
}
if ($edit -eq [IntPtr]::Zero) { throw "filename edit not found" }
# CDM_SETCONTROLTEXT(edt1) is the canonical way to seed a common dialog's filename field
[SV]::SendMessageStr($dlg, 0x0404, [IntPtr]0x047C, $OutFile) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400

# Press the default save button in the dialog
$btn = $dkids | Where-Object { [SV]::Cls($_) -eq "Button" -and [SV]::Text($_) -match "Save" } | Select-Object -First 1
if ($btn) { [SV]::SendMessage($btn, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null }
else { [SV]::SendMessage($dlg, 0x0111, [IntPtr]1, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null }
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1500

# dismiss any info / overwrite message box
foreach ($w in [SV]::TopWindows([uint32]$p.Id)) {
    if ($w -eq $main -or $w -eq $dlg) { continue }
    if ([SV]::Cls($w) -eq "#32770") {
        $mk = [SV]::Kids($w)
        $ok = $mk | Where-Object { [SV]::Cls($_) -eq "Button" } | Select-Object -First 1
        if ($ok) { [SV]::SendMessage($ok, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null }
    }
}
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 700
if (Test-Path $OutFile) { Write-Output "report written: $OutFile ($((Get-Item $OutFile).Length) bytes)" }
else { throw "report file not created" }

param(
    [string]$ProcName = "HyperSense64",
    [string]$OutFile = "",
    [ValidateSet("txt","html")][string]$Format = "txt"
)
# Verifies the SAVE TXT / SAVE HTML buttons through the real UI: mouse-clicks the
# button, types a custom path into the common dialog with the keyboard, presses Enter.
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type @"
using System;using System.Text;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class SV2 {
    public delegate bool EnumProc(IntPtr h, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumChildWindows(IntPtr p, EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr h, out uint pid);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct RECT { public int L,T,R,B; }
    public static string Cls(IntPtr h){var s=new StringBuilder(128);GetClassName(h,s,128);return s.ToString();}
    public static string Txt(IntPtr h){var s=new StringBuilder(512);GetWindowText(h,s,512);return s.ToString();}
    public static List<IntPtr> Kids(IntPtr p){var l=new List<IntPtr>();EnumChildWindows(p,(h,x)=>{l.Add(h);return true;},IntPtr.Zero);return l;}
    public static List<IntPtr> TopWindows(uint pid){var l=new List<IntPtr>();EnumWindows((h,x)=>{uint p;GetWindowThreadProcessId(h,out p);if(p==pid)l.Add(h);return true;},IntPtr.Zero);return l;}
}
"@
if (-not $OutFile) { $OutFile = Join-Path (Get-Location) "tools\report_test.$Format" }

$p = Get-Process $ProcName -ErrorAction Stop | Select-Object -First 1
$main = $p.MainWindowHandle
$kids = [SV2]::Kids($main)

$buttonText = if ($Format -eq "html") { "SAVE HTML" } else { "SAVE TXT" }
$save = $kids | Where-Object { [SV2]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [SV2]::Txt($_) -eq $buttonText } | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $save) { throw "save button not found ($buttonText)" }

# real mouse click - the custom button only notifies on a genuine click
[SV2]::SetForegroundWindow($main) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300
$r = New-Object SV2+RECT
[SV2]::GetWindowRect($save, [ref]$r) | Out-Null
[SV2]::SetCursorPos([int](($r.L+$r.R)/2), [int](($r.T+$r.B)/2)) | Out-Null
[SV2]::mouse_event(2,0,0,0,[IntPtr]::Zero)
[SV2]::mouse_event(4,0,0,0,[IntPtr]::Zero)
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1500

$dlg = [IntPtr]::Zero
foreach ($w in [SV2]::TopWindows([uint32]$p.Id)) {
    if ([SV2]::Cls($w) -eq "#32770" -and [SV2]::Txt($w) -like "*Save HYPERSENSEGLOBAL report*") { $dlg = $w; break }
}
if ($dlg -eq [IntPtr]::Zero) { throw "save dialog not found" }

[SV2]::SetForegroundWindow($dlg) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 600
[System.Windows.Forms.SendKeys]::SendWait("^a")
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 150
[System.Windows.Forms.SendKeys]::SendWait($OutFile)
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300
[System.Windows.Forms.SendKeys]::SendWait("{ENTER}")
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1800

# confirm the overwrite prompt if it appears, then the info box
for ($i = 0; $i -lt 3; $i++) {
    $handled = $false
    foreach ($w in [SV2]::TopWindows([uint32]$p.Id)) {
        if ($w -eq $main -or $w -eq $dlg) { continue }
        if ([SV2]::Cls($w) -eq "#32770") {
            $ok = [SV2]::Kids($w) | Where-Object { [SV2]::Cls($_) -eq "Button" } | Select-Object -First 1
            if ($ok) { [SV2]::SendMessage($ok, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null; $handled = $true }
        }
    }
    if (-not $handled) { break }
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 600
}
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500
if (Test-Path $OutFile) { Write-Output "report written: $OutFile ($((Get-Item $OutFile).Length) bytes)" }
else { throw "report file not created at $OutFile" }

`HyperSense/tools/sign.ps1`:

```ps1
param(
    [string]$Exe = "",
    [string]$Pfx = "",
    [string]$Password = "",
    [string]$Subject = "",
    [string]$Timestamp = "http://timestamp.digicert.com",
    [switch]$NoTimestamp,
    [switch]$VerifyOnly
)
# Signs HyperSense64.exe with Authenticode so SmartScreen / AV vendors can attribute
# the binary to a publisher instead of treating it as anonymous.
#
# Examples
#   .\sign.ps1 -Pfx .\certs\hypersense.pfx -Password 'pfx-password'
#   .\sign.ps1 -Subject "HYPERSENSEGLOBAL"              # cert already in CurrentUser\My
#   .\sign.ps1 -Subject "MyPublisher" -NoTimestamp      # skip the RFC 3161 timestamp
#   .\sign.ps1 -VerifyOnly                              # check the existing signature
#
# Works with or without the Windows SDK: signtool.exe is used when present,
# otherwise the .NET Authenticode API (Set-AuthenticodeSignature) does it.
#
# The PFX password is passed on the command line here for convenience only - on a
# shared machine use an interactive prompt or a hardware token (see README).

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
if (-not $Exe) { $Exe = Join-Path (Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot) "HyperSense64.exe" }
if (-not (Test-Path $Exe)) { throw "executable not found: $Exe" }

$ts = ""
if (-not $NoTimestamp) { $ts = $Timestamp }

function Find-SignTool {
    $t = Get-ChildItem "C:\Program Files (x86)\Windows Kits\10\bin\*\x64\signtool.exe" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
         Sort-Object FullName -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
    if ($t) { return $t.FullName }
    $cmd = Get-Command signtool.exe -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($cmd) { return $cmd.Source }
    return $null
}

function Invoke-SignTool([string]$tool, [string[]]$extra) {
    $params = @("sign", "/fd", "SHA256") + $extra +
              @("/d", "HYPERSENSEGLOBAL Live Usermode Memory Analysis Tool V1")
    if ($ts) { $params += @("/tr", $ts, "/td", "SHA256") }
    $params += $Exe
    & $tool @params
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "signtool sign failed ($LASTEXITCODE)" }
}

$signtool = Find-SignTool

if (-not $VerifyOnly) {
    if ($Pfx) {
        if (-not (Test-Path $Pfx)) { throw "pfx not found: $Pfx" }
        if (-not $signtool) {
            throw "signing from a PFX needs signtool.exe (install the Windows SDK signing tools) " +
                  "or import the certificate into Cert:\CurrentUser\My and use -Subject."
        }
        Invoke-SignTool $signtool @("/f", $Pfx, "/p", $Password)
    }
    elseif ($Subject) {
        $cert = Get-ChildItem Cert:\CurrentUser\My -CodeSigningCert -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
                Where-Object { $_.HasPrivateKey -and $_.Subject -like "*$Subject*" } |
                Sort-Object NotAfter -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
        if (-not $cert) {
            throw "no code-signing certificate with a private key matching '$Subject' in Cert:\CurrentUser\My"
        }
        if ($cert.Subject -eq $cert.Issuer) {
            $warn = "'$($cert.Subject)' is SELF-SIGNED. It only verifies on machines that already trust that " +
                    "certificate. Other PCs, SmartScreen and AV vendors still see an untrusted file - do not " +
                    "expect a VirusTotal false positive to clear from this alone. Use a CA-issued OV/EV " +
                    "certificate for a public release."
            Write-Warning $warn
        }
        if ($signtool) {
            Invoke-SignTool $signtool @("/sha1", $cert.Thumbprint)
        }
        else {
            Write-Output "signtool.exe not found - signing with Set-AuthenticodeSignature instead"
            $signArgs = @{
                FilePath      = $Exe
                Certificate   = $cert
                HashAlgorithm = "SHA256"
            }
            if ($ts) { $signArgs.TimestampServer = $ts }
            $res = Set-AuthenticodeSignature @signArgs
            if ($res.Status -ne "Valid") {
                throw "signing failed: $($res.Status) - $($res.StatusMessage)"
            }
        }
    }
    else {
        throw "pass -Pfx <file.pfx> -Password <pw>, or -Subject <certificate subject>."
    }
}

if ($signtool) {
    & $signtool verify /pa /v $Exe
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "signature verification failed ($LASTEXITCODE)" }
}
else {
    $sig = Get-AuthenticodeSignature $Exe
    if ($sig.Status -ne "Valid") { throw "signature verification failed: $($sig.Status) - $($sig.StatusMessage)" }
    Write-Output ("signed by : " + $sig.SignerCertificate.Subject)
}

Write-Output ("signature OK: " + $Exe)
Write-Output ("SHA-256     : " + (Get-FileHash $Exe -Algorithm SHA256).Hash)

```

`HyperSense/tools/test_allstop.ps1`:

```ps1
param(
    [string]$ProcName = "HyperSense64",
    [int]$RunSeconds = 6
)
Add-Type @"
using System;
using System.Text;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class U3 {
    public delegate bool EnumProc(IntPtr h, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumChildWindows(IntPtr p, EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr h, uint m, IntPtr w, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode, EntryPoint="SendMessageW")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessageStr(IntPtr h, uint m, IntPtr w, StringBuilder l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode, EntryPoint="SendMessageW")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessageText(IntPtr h, uint m, IntPtr w, string l);
    public static List<IntPtr> Kids(IntPtr p){var l=new List<IntPtr>();EnumChildWindows(p,(h,x)=>{l.Add(h);return true;},IntPtr.Zero);return l;}
    public static string Txt(IntPtr h){var s=new StringBuilder(512);GetWindowText(h,s,512);return s.ToString();}
    public static string Cls(IntPtr h){var s=new StringBuilder(128);GetClassName(h,s,128);return s.ToString();}
}
"@

$p = Get-Process $ProcName -ErrorAction Stop | Select-Object -First 1
$main = $p.MainWindowHandle
$kids = [U3]::Kids($main)
$combo = $kids | Where-Object { [U3]::Cls($_) -eq "ComboBox" } | Select-Object -First 1
$search = $kids | Where-Object { [U3]::Cls($_) -eq "Edit" } | Select-Object -First 1

[U3]::SendMessageText($search, 0x000C, [IntPtr]::Zero, "") | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400
[U3]::SendMessage($combo, 0x014E, [IntPtr]1, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
[U3]::SendMessage($main, 0x0111, [IntPtr](100 -bor (1 -shl 16)), $combo) | Out-Null

$run = $kids | Where-Object { [U3]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [U3]::Txt($_) -eq "RUN ANALYSIS" } | Select-Object -First 1
$stop = $kids | Where-Object { [U3]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [U3]::Txt($_) -eq "STOP" } | Select-Object -First 1
[U3]::SendMessage($run, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
Write-Output "run clicked (ALL PROCESSES)"
Start-Sleep -Seconds $RunSeconds

[U3]::SendMessage($stop, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
Write-Output "stop clicked"
Start-Sleep -Seconds 3

$rich = $kids | Where-Object { [U3]::Cls($_) -like "RICHEDIT*" } | Select-Object -First 1
$len = [int][U3]::SendMessage($rich, 0x000E, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero)
$sb = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder ($len + 64)
[U3]::SendMessageStr($rich, 0x000D, [IntPtr]($len + 32), $sb) | Out-Null
$text = $sb.ToString()
Write-Output "outputLines=$(($text -split "`n").Count)"
$text -split "`r`n|`n" | Select-Object -Last 6 | ForEach-Object { Write-Output "  $_" }
if ($text -match "STOPPED") { Write-Output "STOP verified" } else { Write-Output "STOP marker missing" }

```

`HyperSense/tools/test_dialog.ps1`:

```ps1
param([string]$ProcName = "HyperSense64", [switch]$Escape)
# Exercises the custom dark dialog: triggers a validation message, then closes it
# with either the mouse (default) or the ESC key.
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type @"
using System;using System.Text;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class DLG {
    public delegate bool EnumProc(IntPtr h, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumChildWindows(IntPtr p, EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr h, out uint pid);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr h, out RECT r);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr h);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetCursorPos(int x, int y);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern void mouse_event(uint f, uint x, uint y, uint d, IntPtr e);
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct RECT { public int L,T,R,B; }
    public static string Cls(IntPtr h){var s=new StringBuilder(128);GetClassName(h,s,128);return s.ToString();}
    public static string Txt(IntPtr h){var s=new StringBuilder(256);GetWindowText(h,s,256);return s.ToString();}
    public static List<IntPtr> Kids(IntPtr p){var l=new List<IntPtr>();EnumChildWindows(p,(h,x)=>{l.Add(h);return true;},IntPtr.Zero);return l;}
    public static List<IntPtr> Top(uint pid){var l=new List<IntPtr>();EnumWindows((h,x)=>{uint p;GetWindowThreadProcessId(h,out p);if(p==pid)l.Add(h);return true;},IntPtr.Zero);return l;}
}
"@
$p = Get-Process $ProcName -ErrorAction Stop | Select-Object -First 1
$main = $p.MainWindowHandle
$kids = [DLG]::Kids($main)

function Click($hwnd) {
    [DLG]::SetForegroundWindow($main) | Out-Null
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250
    $r = New-Object DLG+RECT
    [DLG]::GetWindowRect($hwnd, [ref]$r) | Out-Null
    [DLG]::SetCursorPos([int](($r.L+$r.R)/2), [int](($r.T+$r.B)/2)) | Out-Null
    [DLG]::mouse_event(2,0,0,0,[IntPtr]::Zero)
    [DLG]::mouse_event(4,0,0,0,[IntPtr]::Zero)
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 600
}

# put the app into REGION mode with no target so RUN raises the info dialog
$region = $kids | Where-Object { [DLG]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [DLG]::Txt($_) -eq "REGION" } | Select-Object -First 1
$run = $kids | Where-Object { [DLG]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [DLG]::Txt($_) -eq "RUN ANALYSIS" } | Select-Object -First 1
Click $region
Click $run
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500

$dlg = [DLG]::Top([uint32]$p.Id) | Where-Object { [DLG]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseDialog" } | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $dlg) { throw "dark dialog did not appear" }
$r = New-Object DLG+RECT
[DLG]::GetWindowRect($dlg, [ref]$r) | Out-Null
Write-Output ("dialog '{0}' at {1}x{2}" -f [DLG]::Txt($dlg), ($r.R-$r.L), ($r.B-$r.T))

$dkids = [DLG]::Kids($dlg)
$ok = $dkids | Where-Object { [DLG]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [DLG]::Txt($_) -eq "OK" } | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $ok) { throw "OK button missing from dialog" }
Write-Output ("dialog children: " + (($dkids | ForEach-Object { [DLG]::Cls($_) }) -join ", "))

if ($Escape) {
    [DLG]::SetForegroundWindow($dlg) | Out-Null
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300
    [System.Windows.Forms.SendKeys]::SendWait("{ESC}")
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800
} else {
    $rr = New-Object DLG+RECT
    [DLG]::GetWindowRect($ok, [ref]$rr) | Out-Null
    [DLG]::SetCursorPos([int](($rr.L+$rr.R)/2), [int](($rr.T+$rr.B)/2)) | Out-Null
    [DLG]::mouse_event(2,0,0,0,[IntPtr]::Zero)
    [DLG]::mouse_event(4,0,0,0,[IntPtr]::Zero)
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800
}

$still = [DLG]::Top([uint32]$p.Id) | Where-Object { [DLG]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseDialog" }
$p.Refresh()
Write-Output ("closed=" + (-not [bool]$still) + " app_responding=" + $p.Responding)

```

`HyperSense/tools/test_region.ps1`:

```ps1
param([string]$ProcName = "HyperSense64")
# Verifies that REGION mode refuses to run without an address, and that a valid
# address is accepted.
Add-Type @"
using System;using System.Text;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class RG {
    public delegate bool EnumProc(IntPtr h, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumChildWindows(IntPtr p, EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr h, out uint pid);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode, EntryPoint="SendMessageW")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessageStr(IntPtr h, uint m, IntPtr w, string l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr h, uint m, IntPtr w, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool IsWindowEnabled(IntPtr h);
    public static string Cls(IntPtr h){var s=new StringBuilder(128);GetClassName(h,s,128);return s.ToString();}
    public static string Txt(IntPtr h){var s=new StringBuilder(512);GetWindowText(h,s,512);return s.ToString();}
    public static List<IntPtr> Kids(IntPtr p){var l=new List<IntPtr>();EnumChildWindows(p,(h,x)=>{l.Add(h);return true;},IntPtr.Zero);return l;}
    public static List<IntPtr> TopWindows(uint pid){var l=new List<IntPtr>();EnumWindows((h,x)=>{uint p;GetWindowThreadProcessId(h,out p);if(p==pid)l.Add(h);return true;},IntPtr.Zero);return l;}
}
"@
$p = Get-Process $ProcName -ErrorAction Stop | Select-Object -First 1
$main = $p.MainWindowHandle
$kids = [RG]::Kids($main)
function Btn($label) { $kids | Where-Object { [RG]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [RG]::Txt($_) -eq $label } | Select-Object -First 1 }

$region = Btn "REGION"
$run = Btn "RUN ANALYSIS"
[RG]::SendMessage($region, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300

$edits = $kids | Where-Object { [RG]::Cls($_) -eq "Edit" }
foreach ($e in $edits) { Write-Output ("edit {0} enabled={1}" -f $e, [RG]::IsWindowEnabled($e)) }

# RUN with an empty address must be blocked by a warning dialog
[RG]::SendMessage($run, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1200
$warn = [IntPtr]::Zero
foreach ($w in [RG]::TopWindows([uint32]$p.Id)) {
    if ([RG]::Cls($w) -eq "#32770") { $warn = $w; break }
}
if ($warn -eq [IntPtr]::Zero) { Write-Output "FAIL: no validation dialog for empty address" }
else {
    Write-Output ("validation dialog: " + [RG]::Txt($warn))
    $ok = [RG]::Kids($warn) | Where-Object { [RG]::Cls($_) -eq "Button" } | Select-Object -First 1
    [RG]::SendMessage($ok, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400
}

# now a valid address should start a scan
$addr = $edits | Select-Object -First 1
[RG]::SendMessageStr($addr, 0x000C, [IntPtr]::Zero, "0x7FFE0000") | Out-Null
$size = $edits | Select-Object -Last 1
[RG]::SendMessageStr($size, 0x000C, [IntPtr]::Zero, "0x1000") | Out-Null
[RG]::SendMessage($run, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
$p.Refresh()
Write-Output ("responding=" + $p.Responding + " exited=" + $p.HasExited)
Write-Output ("run enabled after scan=" + [RG]::IsWindowEnabled($run))

```

`HyperSense/tools/test_search.ps1`:

```ps1
param(
    [string]$Filter = "expl",
    [string]$ProcName = "HyperSense64",
    [int]$WaitSeconds = 60,
    [string]$Pick = "explorer.exe",
    [switch]$NoRun
)

Add-Type @"
using System;
using System.Text;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class U2 {
    public delegate bool EnumProc(IntPtr h, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumChildWindows(IntPtr parent, EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr h, uint msg, IntPtr wp, IntPtr lp);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode, EntryPoint="SendMessageW")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessageStr(IntPtr h, uint msg, IntPtr wp, StringBuilder lp);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode, EntryPoint="SendMessageW")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessageText(IntPtr h, uint msg, IntPtr wp, string lp);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr h, out RECT r);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr h);
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct RECT { public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom; }

    public static List<IntPtr> Children(IntPtr parent) {
        var list = new List<IntPtr>();
        EnumChildWindows(parent, (h, l) => { list.Add(h); return true; }, IntPtr.Zero);
        return list;
    }
    public static string Text(IntPtr h) {
        var sb = new StringBuilder(4096); GetWindowText(h, sb, 4096); return sb.ToString();
    }
    public static string Cls(IntPtr h) {
        var sb = new StringBuilder(128); GetClassName(h, sb, 128); return sb.ToString();
    }
    public static int Top(IntPtr h) {
        RECT r; GetWindowRect(h, out r); return r.Top;
    }
}
"@

$p = Get-Process $ProcName -ErrorAction Stop | Select-Object -First 1
$main = $p.MainWindowHandle
$kids = [U2]::Children($main)

$combo = $kids | Where-Object { [U2]::Cls($_) -eq "ComboBox" } | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $combo) { throw "combo not found" }

# The search box is the top-most VISIBLE plain EDIT child (the classic-mode PID
# override is hidden in normal mode and must never be picked).
$edits = $kids | Where-Object { [U2]::Cls($_) -eq "Edit" -and [U2]::IsWindowVisible($_) } |
         Sort-Object { [U2]::Top($_) }
if (-not $edits) { throw "search edit not found" }
$search = $edits | Select-Object -First 1

[U2]::SendMessageText($search, 0x000C, [IntPtr]::Zero, $Filter) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500

$count = [int][U2]::SendMessage($combo, 0x0146, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero)
$items = @()
for ($i = 0; $i -lt $count; $i++) {
    $sb = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder 512
    [U2]::SendMessageStr($combo, 0x0148, [IntPtr]$i, $sb) | Out-Null
    $items += $sb.ToString()
}
Write-Output "filter='$Filter' comboItems=$count"
$items | ForEach-Object { Write-Output "  [$_]" }

$found = -1
for ($i = 0; $i -lt $count; $i++) { if ($items[$i] -like "*$Pick*") { $found = $i; break } }
if ($found -lt 0) {
    if ($NoRun) { Write-Output "no match for '$Pick'"; return }
    throw "no $Pick in filtered list"
}
if ($NoRun) { Write-Output "match='$Pick' index=$found"; return }

[U2]::SendMessage($combo, 0x014E, [IntPtr]$found, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
[U2]::SendMessage($main, 0x0111, [IntPtr](100 -bor (1 -shl 16)), $combo) | Out-Null
Write-Output "selected=$found ($($items[$found]))"

$run = $kids | Where-Object { [U2]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [U2]::Text($_) -eq "RUN ANALYSIS" } | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $run) { throw "run button not found" }
[U2]::SendMessage($run, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
Write-Output "run clicked"

$rich = $kids | Where-Object { [U2]::Cls($_) -like "RICHEDIT*" } | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $rich) { throw "output pane not found" }

$deadline = (Get-Date).AddSeconds($WaitSeconds)
$text = ""
while ((Get-Date) -lt $deadline) {
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800
    $len = [int][U2]::SendMessage($rich, 0x000E, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero)
    if ($len -le 0) { continue }
    $sb = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder ($len + 32)
    [U2]::SendMessageStr($rich, 0x000D, [IntPtr]($len + 16), $sb) | Out-Null
    $text = $sb.ToString()
    if ($text -match "RESULT") { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 600; break }
}
$lines = $text -split "`r`n|`n"
Write-Output "outputLines=$($lines.Count)"
$lines | Select-Object -Last 14 | ForEach-Object { Write-Output "  $_" }

```

`HyperSense/tools/test_stop.ps1`:

```ps1
Add-Type @"
using System;using System.Text;using System.Collections.Generic;using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class UI2 {
 public delegate bool EnumProc(IntPtr h, IntPtr l);
 [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumChildWindows(IntPtr p, EnumProc cb, IntPtr l);
 [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
 [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
 [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr h, uint m, IntPtr w, IntPtr l);
 public static List<IntPtr> Kids(IntPtr p){var l=new List<IntPtr>();EnumChildWindows(p,(h,x)=>{l.Add(h);return true;},IntPtr.Zero);return l;}
 public static string Text(IntPtr h){var s=new StringBuilder(512);GetWindowText(h,s,512);return s.ToString();}
 public static string Cls(IntPtr h){var s=new StringBuilder(128);GetClassName(h,s,128);return s.ToString();}
}
"@
$p = Get-Process HyperSense64 | Select-Object -First 1
$main = $p.MainWindowHandle
$kids = [UI2]::Kids($main)
$all = $kids | Where-Object { [UI2]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [UI2]::Text($_) -eq "ALL" } | Select-Object -First 1
$run = $kids | Where-Object { [UI2]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [UI2]::Text($_) -eq "RUN ANALYSIS" } | Select-Object -First 1
$stop = $kids | Where-Object { [UI2]::Cls($_) -eq "HyperSenseButton" -and [UI2]::Text($_) -eq "STOP" } | Select-Object -First 1
$combo = $kids | Where-Object { [UI2]::Cls($_) -eq "ComboBox" } | Select-Object -First 1
[UI2]::SendMessage($all, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
[UI2]::SendMessage($combo, 0x014E, [IntPtr]1, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
[UI2]::SendMessage($main, 0x0111, [IntPtr](100 -bor (1 -shl 16)), $combo) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300
[UI2]::SendMessage($run, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
[UI2]::SendMessage($stop, 0x00F5, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
$p.Refresh()
Write-Output "responding=$($p.Responding) exited=$($p.HasExited)"

```
